## FOIL GRIT (2026-09-24)
- User ordered a gritty NFT collection: "not a vanilla bs one" — great graphics, lore, utility, and Foil shilling it. Then: "Release it on Robinhood and base. White list the chads."
- **FOIL GRIT**: 667 supply PER chain (Base + Robinhood Chain = 1,334 total), free mint gas-only, tagline "Every scar is a scam that didn't pay." Same 667 case files on both chains ("two precincts"). All in ~/workspace/nft-god/grit/.
- Contract FoilGrit.sol: ERC-721/721A-style, 667 max, 20 reserve, 2/wallet allowlist, 3/wallet public, Merkle allowlist, ERC-2981 7.5% (cap 10%), Arweave baseURI, one-way freezeURI, renounce after freeze. Compiles (9085B init). Unaudited, undeployed.
- Art: 12 painted bases (4 env × 3 hats, no text baked in) + deterministic code overlays (cred panel, evidence panel with 1 of 40 scam case files, 6 verdict stamps, serial, barcode, grime, vignette). Traits = keccak256(tokenId, "FOIL.GRIT.*"). scripts/compose.mjs built, layout QA passed.
- Allowlist = "the chads": Loopers holders snapshot (Base precinct) + CCFF00 square holders snapshot (Robinhood precinct) + hand-resolved chad wallets. No DM-your-wallet lists.
- scripts/deploy-grit.mjs: compiles, ABI-encodes constructor (verified), dry-run OK (~$0.04 Base). Deploys via Bankr /wallet/submit from Foil's wallet.
- Blockers: bases generating, allowlist snapshots building, Arweave upload needs user's funding approval (fund movement). Then deploy → activate → shill from @Foil667 (standing public-post authority).
- 2026-09-24 ~4:23 PM CDT: QuadGrifters free-claim recon CLOSED as definitively BLOCKED. Free claim = SeaDrop mintSigned requiring a per-wallet ECDSA signature issued only via OpenSea's browser-connected wallet flow; Foil's Bankr-held key cannot do that flow and mandate forbids browser wallet connections. Free window closed 8:28 PM CDT 2026-09-24. Paid 0.004444 stage never touched. Correction: opensea.io/collection/qua is NOT QuadGrifters (it's "QuackQuack"); the real drop page is opensea.io/collection/quadgrifters/overview.
- 2026-09-24 evening: FoilGrit.sol contract test suite green — 56/56 passing on local anvil (constructor/views, phase gating, allowlist incl. root rotation, public phase, reserve, 667 supply boundary, metadata freeze, transfers/approvals, royalty admin, ownership, constructor guards). Test-harness bugs fixed along the way (constructor arg order, bytes4 left-alignment, receipt-status checks, anvil 1.8.3 non-classic mnemonic accounts). Contract still unaudited and undeployed; all GRIT production paused pending user's art-direction answer on 'stock utility'.

## Update — XLords blacklist assessment (03:10 CDT)
- **Replaces any implication that XLords is fully cleared merely because its public mint is price-0/onchain-verified.** Screener blacklist entry `4663:0x384d681c67e78edefde9b53978cae627c15ba870` was added at 02:20 CDT after `rug-check` returned **FLAGGED**.
- Independent rerun against verified source reproduced the result: Slither reported no findings, but heuristics flagged public free minting, owner-controlled minting/supply dilution, owner withdrawal, and runtime `CREATE2` usage. The owner-withdraw flag does not itself endanger funds in a zero-price claim, but owner minting remains a dilution/resale risk and `CREATE2` is a caution.
- Decision: blacklist entry was deliberately left in place; no manual safety override. Any scheduled XLords claim remains subject to the mandatory `claim-with-tinfoil.sh` fail-closed gate and must abort on DANGEROUS, SUSPICIOUS, or scan error.

## 12:04–12:08 CDT — fast-mint repair + DOGEROB attempt (no claim)
- **UPDATE replacing the broken pre-sign flow:** `~/workspace/nft-god/fast-mint/fast-mint.mjs` was rewritten to use Bankr `POST /wallet/submit` at fire time (`waitForConfirmation:false`) instead of unsupported `/wallet/sign` `eth_signTransaction` + raw-RPC broadcast/RBF. It now uses the canonical SeaDrop fee recipient `0x0000a26b00c1f0df003000390027140000faa719`, polls receipts read-only, verifies the returned signer/tx hash, and handles both forms of Bankr 403 rejection without retrying. Pre-open encoding remains, but nothing is signed until submission.
- Dry tests passed fail-closed behavior without signing/submitting: KAGEURI was rejected because price was nonzero; MALS was rejected as an inactive/non-SeaDrop target; the 132-byte `mintPublic` calldata and canonical fee-recipient encoding were independently checked.
- Headless radar build `1790355834179` returned 185 cards. Immediate free-public leads included DOGEROB (`0x7d5b49859fc151c764e6fbac938c1bf4a0fe6991`, public free ×1000 opening 12:08:11 CDT), ROOST (12:15 CDT), and 0xQUIT SAVES THE DAY (12:30 CDT). Existing CREDITDOGS and GROKS staging remains pending.
- DOGEROB’s OpenSea collection URL hit the same generic OpenSea-bundle Tinfoil verdict already observed elsewhere (`SUSPICIOUS`: EIP-7702/obfuscation patterns). The collection contract itself is a 45-byte EIP-1167 minimal proxy with a hardcoded implementation shared by known Robinhood SeaDrop clones, not a swappable unknown delegatecall target.
- **Rug-check correction:** `rug-check.mjs` had systematically misclassified EIP-1167 minimal proxies as HIGH `DELEGATECALL`. It was updated to recognize the tiny standard minimal-proxy pattern as INFO; DOGEROB was removed from `blacklist.json` and rescanned CLEAN (bytecode-only, reduced confidence). Only DOGEROB’s newly added false-positive entry was removed.
- A live DOGEROB claim was staged through the repaired pipeline, but `--early-ms 750` caused fire-time verification at 12:08:10 CDT—one second before the 12:08:11 start—so it correctly aborted as not active. **No transaction was signed or submitted; no DOGEROB landed.** A post-open retry was not completed before interruption and remains pending.

## Flip desk 18:36 CDT — first listings live
- Listed jean-phil-hood #102 and #103 on OpenSea (Robinhood Chain) at 0.00059 ETH (~$1.59) each, 30-day Seaport orders. #102: 0x040cc53e2c6867cc12ab81341f45b181b2065b877be7063dc3db2f333daba8a8 (ACTIVE). #103 confirmed in orderbook.
- Required a setApprovalForAll to OpenSea's RH conduit (0x963f00d3ff000064ffcba824b800c0000000c300) for the jean-phil-hood collection — tx 0x791778876da657bb7be0c1c2a52ebd6c3b3084e72e23033efcd8e037f2e4c292, gas $0.0076 (well under $0.50 cap). Without it OpenSea rejects: "ERC721 conduit not approved".
- Key discovery: Robinhood's Seaport is a CUSTOM deployment at 0x0000000000000068F116a894984e2DB1123eB395 (not the canonical address). Its dispatcher lacks getCounter/eip712Domain but has fulfill functions; standard EIP-712 (Seaport/1.6) signatures validate fine on OpenSea.
- Also learned: eth_gasPrice on Robinhood RPC returns bogus 34.8 gwei; real baseFee is ~0.04 gwei (confirmed from recent blocks). Always cross-check before costing.
- Everything else in inventory is below the $1 dust line. Protected: CCFF00 #4429, TBA (0x5547DCE634e1cdFE1f7E1b9038B50a5d98eA914E, untouched, 0 NFTs), all Loopers, Helixa #5290.
